```
def fast_conv_self(a):
   n=len(a); size=2*n-1; fsize=1; while fsize<size: fsize*=2; A=fft(a, fsize); c=real(ifft(A*A)); c[:size]
```

```
def run_softmax_adam(a_init, time_budget, seed=0):
    a = np.asarray(a_init,float); n=len(a)
    if n <2 or time_budget <=0: return a, _fast_objective(a)
    S = np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a = np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s <1e-12: a=np.ones(n)
    a = a*s? To normalize to S: a = a*(S/s)
    amin = 1e-12 * max(1.0, a.max())
    a = a + amin
    S = a.sum()
    theta = np.log(a) - np.log(S)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    best_a=a.copy(); best_val=_fast_objective(a) # ratio
    start=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_budget:
       t+=1; prog=min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-start)/time_budget)
       a = _softmax_theta(theta, S)
       c = _fast_conv_self(a)
       cmax = c.max()
       cur = 2*n*cmax/(a.sum()**2)
       if cur < best_val: best_val=cur; best_a=a.copy()
       T = 2.0 + 1500.0*prog**1.7 # high
       w = _soft_weights(c,T)
       grad_a = _grad_a_from_weights(w,a)
       dot = np.dot(grad_a,a)
       grad = grad_a*a - (a*dot)/S
       gn=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if not finite or gn <1e-16: break
       # Adam
       ... lr = 0.05 * (0.5 ** prog)
       theta = theta - lr*(m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+1e-8))
       # Occasionally project theta to prevent too large? softmax stable.
    return best_a,best_val
```

```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft

def _next_pow2(x):
    if x <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
```

`_fast_conv_self`:
```
def _fast_conv_self(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(a)
    if n < 64:
       c=np.convolve(a,a,mode='full')
    else:
       size=2*n-1
       fsize=_next_pow2(size)
       A=fft(a, fsize)
       c=np.real(ifft(A*A))
       c = c[:size]
       # remove small negative
       # c[np.abs(c)<1e-12]=0? no
       c = np.where(c > 0, c, 0.0) # might remove negative noise but also true negative impossible for nonneg a; if FFT negative small only. If some true due precision? Nonneg convolution nonnegative, so okay. But if input has negative? We clip before. Good.
    return c
```
Using `np.where(c>0,c,0)` may clip negative large if a had tiny negative? We ensure nonneg. Good.
But `fft` of float64 yields complex128. `A*A` not conjugate (convolution, not correlation), correct.

```
def _fast_objective(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size==0: return inf
   a=np.clip(a,0,None)
   s=a.sum()
   if s < 1e-12: return inf
   c=_fast_conv_self(a)
   if c.size==0: return inf
   m=c.max()
   if not np.isfinite(m): return inf
   return float(2.0*a.size*m/(s*s))
```

`_clean_seq_for_eval`:
```
def _clean_for_eval(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).reshape(-1)
   if a.size==0: return None
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   if a.max()>1000: a = a * (1000.0/a.max())
   if a.sum() < 0.01:
       a=np.maximum(a,0.0)+0.01/float(a.size)
   return a
```
But scale if max>1000: if max finite. We also clip negative. This changes shape if had negatives (clipping to0 then sum maybe). For candidates scaled max1, fine.

```
def _try_candidate(a, label, best_val, best_a):
   arr=_clean_for_eval(a)
   if arr is None: return best_val,best_a
   try:
      val=evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())
   except Exception:
      val=np.inf
   if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
      print(...)
      return float(val), arr.copy()
   return best_val,best_a
```

`_scale_shape`:
```
def _scale_shape(y):
   y=np.asarray(y,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
   if y.size==0: return None
   y=np.where(np.isfinite(y),y,0.0)
   y=np.clip(y,0.0,None)
   ymax=float(y.max())
   if ymax <= 0 or not np.isfinite(ymax):
      return np.ones(y.size)
   y = y/ymax
   # add epsilon and rescale? Adding could make negative? no.
   eps=1e-12
   y = y + eps
   y = y/y.max()
   if y.sum() < 1e-12:
      return np.ones(y.size)
   return y
```

Candidate list:
```
def _make_parametric_candidates(n, deadline=None):
    res=[]
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    if n>1: x = x/float(n-1)
    else: x=np.array([0.0])
    # clamped slopes
    for c in [...]:
       y=1.0 + c*(2.0*x-1.0)
       res.append(_scale_shape(np.maximum(y,0.0)))
       y=1.0 - c*(2.0*x-1.0)  # equivalent other orientation
       res.append(_scale_shape(np.maximum(y,0.0)))
    # pure ramps
    res.append(_scale_shape(x + 1e-12))
    res.append(_scale_shape(1.0 - x +1e-12))
    # power ramp
    for p in [...]:
       res.append(_scale_shape(np.power(np.maximum(x,0.0), p)+1e-12))
       res.append(_scale_shape(np.power(np.maximum(1.0-x,0.0), p)+1e-12))
    # uniform
    res.append(_scale_shape(np.ones(n)))
    # midpoint sine support? maybe
    for p in [1.0,2.0,3.0]:
       res.append(_scale_shape(np.sin(np.pi*x)**p))
    return res
```
But for n=4096 and many shape (2*10+2+16+2+3=33). Good.

Oscillatory:
```
def _make_osc_candidates(n):
   res=[]
   x=np.arange(n)/float(n) # or n-1
   freqs=[3,7,15,31,63,127,255,511,1023]
   for m in freqs:
       if m >= n//2: continue
       for amp in [1.0,0.6]:
          for ph in [0.0, 0.5, 1.0]:
             ang = 2*np.pi*m*x + np.pi*ph
             y=1.0 + amp*np.sin(ang)
             res.append(_scale_shape(np.maximum(y,0.0)))
             y=1.0 + amp*np.cos(ang)
             res.append(_scale_shape(np.maximum(y,0.0)))
   # square envelope
   for m in [7,63,511]:
       if m >= n//2: continue
       ang=2*np.pi*m*x
       y=(1.0+np.sin(ang))**2
       res.append(_scale_shape(y))
       y=(1.0-np.cos(ang))**2
       res.append(_scale_shape(y))
   return res
```

```
def _slope_objective(n, sign, c):
    x=np.arange(n)/float(n-1) if n>1 else np.array([0.0])
    y=np.maximum(1.0 + sign*c*(2.0*x -1.0),0.0)
    a=_scale_shape(y)
    return _fast_objective(a)
```

```
def _lp_improve(a, M=None, top_k=200, max_time=10):
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    n=len(a)
    c=_fast_conv_self(a); M=c.max()
    # choose active_lags top top_k, but if many near max include threshold
    active = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
    A_rows=[]
    for k in active:
        lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
        if lo<=hi:
           row=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
           # row[lo:hi+1] = a[k - np.arange(lo,hi+1)]
           idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
           row[idx] = a[k-idx]
           A_rows.append(row)
    A = np.vstack(A_rows)
    # use sparse? For 200x4096 dense okay. Dense may be faster for HiGHS? Could be.
    c_obj=-np.ones(n)
    b=np.full(len(active), M)
    res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'time_limit':max_time,'presolve':True})
    if success...
```

`run_adam`:
```
def run_adam(a_init, time_budget, T, lr0=0.05, verbose=True):
   a=np.asarray(a_init,float); n=len(a)
   if time_budget <=0 or n<2: return a, _fast_objective(a)
   S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
   a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12: a=np.ones(n)
   a=a*(S/s)
   eps=1e-12*max(1.0,a.max())
   p=a/S; p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); p=p/p.sum()
   theta=np.log(p)
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
   best_a=a.copy(); best_val=_fast_objective(a)
   start=time.monotonic()
   while time.monotonic()-start < time_budget:
      t+=1; progress=min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start)/time_budget)
      a=_softmax_theta(theta,S)
      c=_fast_conv_self(a)
      cmax=float(c.max())
      cur=float(2.0*n*cmax/(a.sum()**2))
      if cur<best_val: ...
      w=_soft_weights(c,T)
      ga=_grad_from_weights(w,a)
      dot=float(np.dot(ga,a))
      grad=ga*a - (a*dot)/S
      gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
      if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm<1e-16: break
      m=0.85*m+0.15*grad
      v=0.999*v+0.001*(grad*grad)
      mhat=m/(1-0.85**t)
      vhat=v/(1-0.999**t)
      lr=lr0*(0.6 ** progress)
      theta=theta - lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
   return best_a,best_val
```
Use `0.85**t` each step slow? t maybe 10000, pow okay. Could keep decay powers incrementally? Not needed. But Python pow 10000 negligible. Better beta1^t. Use variables `p1=0.85**t` maybe pow.
Could avoid recomputing `a.sum()` each step; S fixed but softmax sum S up to rounding. Use S.

```
def _golden_shift(a, basis, S, cap, iters=8):

    for it in range(iters):

          hi=x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=lo+c*(hi-lo); f1=...
       else:
          lo=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=lo+d*(hi-lo); f2=...
    best_x = x1 if f1<f2 else x2
    best_f=min(f1,f2)
```

Detailed `_lp_polish`:
```
def _lp_polish(a_init, time_limit=60):
   a=normalize; S=...
   best=a; best_val=...
   for attempt in range(2):
      if time.time > time_limit: break
      c=conv(a); M=c.max()
      K = 200 if attempt==0 else 350
      n=len(a); active=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
      A=np.zeros((K,n))
      for r,k in enumerate(active): ...
      if not np.any(np.isfinite(A)): break
      b=np.full(K, M*(1+1e-8)+1e-12)
      cvec=-np.ones(n)
      bounds=[(0,1.0)]*n
      try: res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': max(1, time_left)})
      except Exception: break
      if res.success and res.x is not None:
         g=res.x.astype(float); gs=g.sum()
         if gs>1e-6:
            g=g*(S/gs)
            # add g and blends
            for t in [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9,1.0]: ...
            a=best; # for next attempt use best
   return best,best_val
```

Now code details for helper `_next_pow2`:

```
def _next_pow2(x):
    x=int(x)
    if x <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()
```

```
def _fft_convolve(u,v):
    out_len=len(u)+len(v)-1
    fsize=_next_pow2(out_len)
    U=fft(u,fsize); V=fft(v,fsize)
    c=np.real(ifft(U*V))
    return c[:out_len]
```

```
def _make_seed_arrays(n, rng_seed=42):
    res=[]
    if n < 2: res.append(np.ones(n)); return res
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n-1)
    res.append(_scale_shape(np.ones(n)))
    # polynomials
    for p in [0.5,0.8,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0]:
       res.append(_scale_shape(np.power(x,p)+1e-9))
       res.append(_scale_shape(np.power(1-x,p)+1e-9))
    # exponentials
    for al in [-2,-1,1,2]:
       res.append(_scale_shape(np.exp(al*x)))
    # symmetric humps
    for m in [1,2,3,5,8,13]:
       for amp in [0.5,1.0]:
          res.append(_scale_shape(np.maximum(1.0+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x + np.pi/2? maybe),0)))
          res.append(_scale_shape(np.maximum(1.0+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x),0)))
    # frequency squares
    # random smooth
    rng=np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    for r in range(6):
        theta=np.zeros(n)
        for m in range(1,8):
            c=rng.normal(0,0.7); d=rng.normal(0,0.7)
            theta += c*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x) + d*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x)
        res.append(_scale_shape(np.exp(theta - theta.max())))
    return res
```

`_soft_weights`: If `c` has negative max? no.
```
def _soft_weights(c,T):
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0 or not np.isfinite(cmax): return np.full(c.size,1/c.size)
    scaled = T*(c-cmax)
    # scaled max 0
    scaled = scaled - np.max(scaled) # max 0 to guard
    e=np.exp(scaled)
    s=e.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0:
       w=np.zeros(c.size); idx=np.argmax(c); w[idx]=1; return w
    return e/s
```
If T large and all scaled -inf except max, sum=1.

```
grad = np.zeros(n)
grad[:end-start] = 2*conv[start:min(end,len(conv))]
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Strategy:
- Start from the previously found construction if available.
- Maintain a pool of same-length candidates.
- Use fast FFT convexity-constrained local tools:
  * Fourier-mode golden line searches
  * a limited LP directional move inspired by the SoTA description
  * Adam on log-softmax parameters with temperature-annealed soft-max
- Always verify the final answer with evaluate_sequence.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_convolve(u, v):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = len(u) + len(v) - 1
    fsize = _next_pow2(out_len)
    U = fft(u, fsize)
    V = fft(v, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(U * V))
    return c[:out_len]


def _fast_conv_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n < 256:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
        c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
        return c

    size = 2 * n - 1
    fsize = _next_pow2(size)
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    c = c[:size]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c


def _fast_objective(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    c = _fast_conv_self(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _scale_shape(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if y.size == 0:
        return None
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, None)
    ymax = float(y.max())
    if ymax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ymax):
        return np.ones(y.size, dtype=np.float64)
    y = y / ymax
    y = y + 1e-12
    y = y / float(y.max())
    if y.sum() <= 1e-14:
        return np.ones(y.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return y


def _normalize_to_length_sum(a, target_sum):
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return None
    return a * (target_sum / s)


def _try_candidate(a, label, best_val, best_a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0 or a.size > 16384:
        return best_val, best_a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.max() > 1000.0:
        a = a * (1000.0 / a.max())
    if a.sum() < 0.01:
        a = a + 0.01 / float(a.size)

    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
    except Exception:
        val = np.inf

    if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
        print("new best [%s]: val=%.8f n=%d" % (label, val, a.size))
        return float(val), a.copy()
    return best_val, best_a


def _add_pool(pool_vals, pool, a, n_target, label=""):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size != n_target:
        return pool_vals, pool
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return pool_vals, pool

    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n_target))
    a = a * (S / s)
    val = _fast_objective(a)
    if np.isfinite(val):
        pool_vals.append(float(val))
        pool.append(a.copy())
        if len(pool) <= 40 or val < 1.52:
            print("pool [%s]: internal=%.8f" % (label, val))
    return pool_vals, pool


def _make_seed_arrays(n):
    res = []
    if n < 2:
        res.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        return res

    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n - 1))
    res.append(_scale_shape(np.ones(n)))

    for p in [0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.3, 1.6, 2.0, 3.0]:
        res.append(_scale_shape(np.power(np.maximum(x, 0.0), p) + 1e-9))
        res.append(_scale_shape(np.power(np.maximum(1.0 - x, 0.0), p) + 1e-9))

    for al in [-3.0, -1.5, -0.7, 0.7, 1.5, 3.0]:
        res.append(_scale_shape(np.exp(al * x) + 1e-9))

    for m in [1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, 21]:
        for amp in [0.4, 0.7, 1.0]:
            res.append(_scale_shape(np.maximum(1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x), 0.0)))
            res.append(_scale_shape(np.maximum(1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x), 0.0)))
            res.append(_scale_shape(np.maximum(1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * (x + 0.25)), 0.0)))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    for _ in range(8):
        theta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 9):
            c = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7)
            d = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7)
            theta += c * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x)
            theta += d * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x)
        res.append(_scale_shape(np.exp(theta - theta.max())))

    return res


def _softmax_theta(theta, S):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    t = theta - float(np.max(theta))
    e = np.exp(t)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(theta.size, S / float(theta.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return S * e / s


def _soft_weights(c, T):
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
    scaled = T * (c - cmax)
    scaled = scaled - float(np.max(scaled))
    e = np.exp(scaled)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def _grad_from_weights(w, a):
    n = a.size
    if n == 1:
        return 2.0 * float(w[0]) * a.copy()
    rev = a[::-1]
    full = _fft_convolve(w, rev)
    start = n - 1
    end = start + n
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    stop = min(end, full.size)
    grad[:stop - start] = 2.0 * full[start:stop]
    return grad


def _run_adam_soft(a_init, time_budget, temp, lr0=0.025):
    a = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time_budget <= 0.0:
        return a, _fast_objective(a)

    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    a = a * (S / s)

    amin = 1e-12 * max(1.0, float(a.max()))
    p = a / S
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    p = p / float(p.sum())
    theta = np.log(p)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    beta1, beta2 = 0.85, 0.999
    eps = 1e-8

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_val = _fast_objective(a)
    start = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - start < time_budget:
        t += 1
        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / max(1e-9, time_budget))

        a = _softmax_theta(theta, S)
        c = _fast_conv_self(a)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        cur = float(2.0 * n * cmax / (float(a.sum()) ** 2))
        if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < best_val:
            best_val = cur
            best_a = a.copy()

        w = _soft_weights(c, temp)
        ga = _grad_from_weights(w, a)
        dot = float(np.dot(ga, a))
        grad = ga * a - (a * dot) / S

        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gn) or gn < 1e-16:
            break

        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * grad
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (grad * grad)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - beta1 ** t)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - beta2 ** t)

        lr = lr0 * (0.55 ** progress)
        theta = theta - lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)

    return best_a, best_val


def _shift_array(a, basis, delta, S):
    b = a + delta * basis
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return None
    return b * (S / s)


def _golden_shift_eval(a, basis, S, cap, iters):
    lo = -cap
    hi = cap
    if hi <= lo:
        return None, _fast_objective(a), 0.0

    resphi = (np.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    c = 1.0 - resphi
    d = resphi

    x1 = lo + c * (hi - lo)
    x2 = lo + d * (hi - lo)

    arr1 = _shift_array(a, basis, x1, S)
    f1 = _fast_objective(arr1) if arr1 is not None else np.inf
    arr2 = _shift_array(a, basis, x2, S)
    f2 = _fast_objective(arr2) if arr2 is not None else np.inf

    for _ in range(max(0, int(iters))):
        if f1 < f2:
            hi = x2
            x2 = x1
            f2 = f1
            x1 = lo + c * (hi - lo)
            arr1 = _shift_array(a, basis, x1, S)
            f1 = _fast_objective(arr1) if arr1 is not None else np.inf
        else:
            lo = x1
            x1 = x2
            f1 = f2
            x2 = lo + d * (hi - lo)
            arr2 = _shift_array(a, basis, x2, S)
            f2 = _fast_objective(arr2) if arr2 is not None else np.inf

    if f1 <= f2:
        bd = x1
        bf = f1
    else:
        bd = x2
        bf = f2

    ba = _shift_array(a, basis, bd, S)
    if ba is None:
        return a, _fast_objective(a), 0.0
    return ba, float(bf), float(bd)


def _fourier_polish(a_init, time_budget, verbose=True):
    a = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time_budget <= 0.0:
        return a, _fast_objective(a)

    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    a = a * (S / s)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_val = _fast_objective(a)
    if verbose:
        print("fourier polish start: %.8f" % best_val)

    if time_budget > 120.0:
        max_modes = 56
        cap_seq = [0.05, 0.12, 0.25, 0.5]
        iters = 8
    elif time_budget > 45.0:
        max_modes = 32
        cap_seq = [0.1, 0.25, 0.5]
        iters = 6
    else:
        max_modes = 14
        cap_seq = [0.2, 0.5]
        iters = 5

    mean_a = S / float(n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    for cap_factor in cap_seq:
        if time.monotonic() - elapsed > time_budget:
            break
        cap = max(1e-12, cap_factor * mean_a)
        for mode in range(1, max_modes + 1):
            if time.monotonic() - elapsed > time_budget:
                break

            for kind in (0, 1):
                if time.monotonic() - elapsed > time_budget:
                    break
                if kind == 0:
                    basis = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * mode * x)
                else:
                    basis = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * mode * x)

                ba, bf, bd = _golden_shift_eval(a, basis, S, cap, iters)
                if ba is not None and np.isfinite(bf) and bf < best_val - 1e-12:
                    a = ba
                    best_val = bf
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    if verbose:
                        print("  fourier improve mode=%d kind=%d delta=%.4g val=%.8f" %
                              (mode, kind, bd, best_val))

        if best_val < best_val - 1e-12:
            pass
    return best_a, best_val


def _lp_polish(a_init, time_budget):
    a = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time_budget <= 1.0 or linprog is None:
        return a, _fast_objective(a)

    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    a = a * (S / s)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_val = _fast_objective(a)
    start = time.monotonic()

    for attempt in range(2):
        if time.monotonic() - start > time_budget:
            break

        c = _fast_conv_self(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0:
            break

        K = 180 if attempt == 0 else 320
        K = max(20, min(K, c.size))
        if K == c.size:
            active = np.arange(c.size)
        else:
            active = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]

        A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for r, k in enumerate(active):
            lo = max(0, int(k) - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, int(k))
            if lo <= hi:
                A[r, lo:hi + 1] = a[int(k) - np.arange(lo, hi + 1)]

        b = np.full(K, M * (1.0 + 1e-7) + 1e-12, dtype=np.float64)
        cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

        remaining = max(0.0, time_budget - (time.monotonic() - start))
        try:
            res = linprog(
                cvec,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"time_limit": int(max(1, min(45, remaining))), "presolve": True}
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False) is True and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
            gs = float(g.sum())
            if gs > 1e-8:
                g = g * (S / gs)
                gv = _fast_objective(g)
                if np.isfinite(gv) and gv < best_val - 1e-10:
                    best_val = gv
                    best_a = g.copy()

                for t in [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.45, 0.6, 0.8, 1.0]:
                    cand = (1.0 - t) * best_a + t * g
                    cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
                    cs = float(cand.sum())
                    if cs > 1e-12:
                        cand = cand * (S / cs)
                        cv = _fast_objective(cand)
                        if np.isfinite(cv) and cv < best_val - 1e-10:
                            best_val = cv
                            best_a = cand.copy()

    return best_a, best_val


def _blend_pool(pool, n_target, time_budget, verbose=True):
    if len(pool) < 2 or time_budget <= 1.0:
        return pool

    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n_target))
    start = time.monotonic()
    original_limit = min(8, len(pool))
    alphas = [0.1, 0.25, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.75, 0.9]

    added = 0
    for i in range(original_limit):
        if time.monotonic() - start > time_budget:
            break
        ai = np.asarray(pool[i], dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(i + 1, original_limit):
            if time.monotonic() - start > time_budget:
                break
            aj = np.asarray(pool[j], dtype=np.float64)
            for t in alphas:
                if time.monotonic() - start > time_budget:
                    break
                b = (1.0 - t) * ai + t * aj
                b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
                s = float(b.sum())
                if s <= 1e-14:
                    continue
                b = b * (S / s)
                val = _fast_objective(b)
                if np.isfinite(val) and val < min(pool_vals_global) - 1e-10:
                    pool.append(b.copy())
                    pool_vals_global.append(float(val))
                    added += 1
                    if verbose and added <= 12:
                        print("blend improve: val=%.8f" % val)
    return pool


# A tiny global auxiliary list used only by _blend_pool to avoid extra return plumb.
pool_vals_global = []


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    global pool_vals_global

    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(total_budget) or total_budget <= 0.0:
        total_budget = 2400.0
    total_budget = min(total_budget, 2400.0)

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(10.0, total_budget - 30.0)

    best_val = np.inf
    best_a = None

    # Load parent construction if present.
    given_arr = None
    try:
        raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if raw.size >= 16 and raw.sum() > 0.0:
            given_arr = raw
            if raw.size <= 16384:
                best_val, best_a = _try_candidate(raw, "given", best_val, best_a)
            else:
                # Downsample very long parents to a manageable length.
                n_ds = 4096
                idx = np.linspace(0, raw.size - 1, n_ds).astype(int)
                ds = np.clip(raw, 0.0, None)
                ds = ds[idx]
                if ds.sum() > 0.0:
                    best_val, best_a = _try_candidate(ds, "given_downsample", best_val, best_a)
    except Exception:
        given_arr = None

    # Decide search length. Use the parent if compatible, otherwise 4096.
    n_search = 4096
    if best_a is not None and 256 <= len(best_a) <= 4096:
        n_search = len(best_a)
    elif given_arr is not None and 256 <= len(given_arr) <= 4096:
        n_search = len(given_arr)

    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n_search))

    # Candidate pool for internal fast optimization. Use same length only.
    pool_vals = []
    pool = []

    if given_arr is not None:
        if len(given_arr) == n_search:
            pool_vals, pool = _add_pool(pool_vals, pool, given_arr, n_search, "given_pool")
        else:
            idx = np.linspace(0, len(given_arr) - 1, n_search).astype(int)
            g = np.clip(given_arr, 0.0, None)
            pool_vals, pool = _add_pool(pool_vals, pool, g[idx], n_search, "given_ds_pool")

    # Add analytic seeds.
    seeds_done = 0
    for arr in _make_seed_arrays(n_search):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90.0:
            break
        pool_vals, pool = _add_pool(pool_vals, pool, arr, n_search, "seed%d" % seeds_done)
        seeds_done += 1

    # Exact-evaluate a handful of the best internal candidates.
    if pool:
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
            order = np.argsort(pool_vals)
            max_exact = 25
            if deadline - time.monotonic() < 120.0:
                max_exact = 8 if len(order) > 8 else len(order)
            max_exact = max(3, min(max_exact, len(order)))
            for ii in range(max_exact):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
                    break
                idx = int(order[ii])
                best_val, best_a = _try_candidate(pool[idx], "pool_internal_top", best_val, best_a)

    # Choose starting point for local optimization.
    opt_a = None
    if best_a is not None and len(best_a) == n_search:
        opt_a = best_a
    elif pool:
        idx0 = int(np.argmin(pool_vals))
        opt_a = pool[idx0]
    else:
        opt_a = np.ones(n_search, dtype=np.float64)

    if opt_a is None:
        opt_a = np.ones(n_search, dtype=np.float64)

    # Fourier-mode polish first: cheap, often improves smooth basins.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        ft_budget = min(210.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.55)
        a_f, v_f = _fourier_polish(opt_a, ft_budget)
        if v_f < _fast_objective(opt_a):
            opt_a, best_candidate_val = a_f, v_f
            pool_vals, pool = _add_pool(pool_vals, pool, a_f, n_search, "fourier")
        else:
            best_candidate_val = v_f
        best_val, best_a = _try_candidate(a_f, "fourier", best_val, best_a)

    # LP directional move, inspired by the LP approach in the SoTA description.
    if linprog is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        lp_budget = min(140.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.45)
        a_l, v_l = _lp_polish(opt_a, lp_budget)
        if np.isfinite(v_l) and v_l < _fast_objective(opt_a):
            opt_a = a_l
        pool_vals, pool = _add_pool(pool_vals, pool, a_l, n_search, "lp")
        best_val, best_a = _try_candidate(a_l, "lp", best_val, best_a)

    # Soft-max Adam refines active-set sensitivity. Use several temperatures.
    for temp, frac, lr in [(400.0, 0.25, 0.03), (1500.0, 0.20, 0.02), (5000.0, 0.15, 0.012)]:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 45.0:
            break
        ad_budget = min(90.0, max(20.0, remaining * frac))
        a_ad, v_ad = _run_adam_soft(opt_a, ad_budget, temp, lr0=lr)
        if np.isfinite(v_ad) and v_ad < _fast_objective(opt_a):
            opt_a = a_ad
        pool_vals, pool = _add_pool(pool_vals, pool, a_ad, n_search, "adam_T%d" % int(temp))
        best_val, best_a = _try_candidate(a_ad, "adam_T%d" % int(temp), best_val, best_a)

    # Blend the best internal candidates; simple averaging can sometimes lower peaks.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0 and len(pool) >= 2:
        # Use a temporary global to allow _blend_pool to update many values efficiently.
        saved_vals = pool_vals_global[:]
        pool_vals_global[:] = list(pool_vals)
        try:
            blend_budget = min(120.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.35)
            pool = _blend_pool(pool, n_search, blend_budget)
            pool_vals = list(pool_vals_global)
            if pool_vals:
                jb = int(np.argmin(pool_vals))
                best_val, best_a = _try_candidate(pool[jb], "pool_blend_best", best_val, best_a)
        finally:
            pool_vals_global[:] = saved_vals

    # Final exact verification and safety fallback.
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

    best_val, best_a = _try_candidate(best_a, "final", best_val, best_a)

    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_val):
        fallback = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        fallback_list = fallback.tolist()
        try:
            fb = evaluate_sequence(fallback_list)
        except Exception:
            fb = np.inf
        print("Fallback obj:", fb)
        return fallback_list

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    best_a = np.where(np.isfinite(best_a), best_a, 0.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if best_a.sum() < 0.01:
        best_a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

    final_list = best_a.tolist()
    try:
        final_val = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
    except Exception:
        final_val = np.inf

    if not np.isfinite(final_val):
        fallback_list = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64).tolist()
        try:
            fb = evaluate_sequence(fallback_list)
        except Exception:
            fb = np.inf
        print("FINAL fallback:", fb)
        return fallback_list

    print("FINAL verified objective: %.8f (n=%d)" % (final_val, len(final_list)))
    return final_list
```
